<script type="text/javascript">
    function homeslider() {
        $('#home-slider .owl-carousel').owlCarousel({
            loop: true,
            autoplay: true,
            responsiveClass: true,
            dots: true,
            navRewind: true,
            nav: true,
            items: 1,
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        homeslider();
        if (jQuery.browser.webkit) {
            $(window).resize(function () {
                $('.list-view').trigger('destroy.owl.carousel').removeClass('owl-loaded owl-rtl owl-loaded owl-drag');
                $('.list-view').find('.owl-stage-outer').children().unwrap();
                listCarousel();

                $('#home-slider .owl-carousel').trigger('destroy.owl.carousel').removeClass('owl-loaded owl-rtl owl-loaded owl-drag');
                $('#home-slider .owl-carousel').find('.owl-stage-outer').children().unwrap();
                homeslider();
            });
        }
    });

</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    function listCarousel() {
        $('.list-view').owlCarousel({
            rtl: true,
            loop: false,
            margin: 30,
            responsiveClass: true,
            dots: false,
            navRewind: false,
            nav: true,
            lazyLoad: true,
            responsive: {
                0: {
                    items: 2,
                },
                600: {
                    items: 4,
                },
                1000: {
                    items: 5,
                }
            },
            onLoadedLazy: removeimageloader
        });
    }
    function removeimageloader(e) {
        jQuery("img[src='" + e.url + "']").removeClass('imageloader');
    }




    $(document).ready(function () {
        $('#home-slider .rating-loading').rating({'size': 'x1', 'showClear': false, 'showCaption': false, 'rtl': true});
        // Password confirm
        if (confirm == true) {
            swal({
                title: "تم التفعيل!",
                text: "تم تأكيد بريدك الإلكتروني وتفعيل حسابك بنجاح.",
                type: "success",
                allowOutsideClick: true,
                confirmButtonClass: "btn-success",
                confirmButtonText: "إغلاق",
            });
        } else if (confirm == false) {
            swal({
                title: "لم يتم التفعيل!",
                text: "عذراً لم يتم تأكيد بريدك الإلكتروني",
                type: "error",
                allowOutsideClick: true,
                confirmButtonClass: "btn-success",
                confirmButtonText: "إغلاق",
            });
        }
        // Password reset
        if (reset_pass == true) {
            swal({
                title: "كلمة المرور",
                text: "تم إعادة ضبط كلمة المرور بنجاح!",
                type: "success",
                allowOutsideClick: true,
                confirmButtonClass: "btn-success",
                confirmButtonText: "إغلاق",
            });
        }

        listCarousel();
        if (jQuery.browser.webkit) {
            $(window).resize(function () {
                $('.list-view').trigger('destroy.owl.carousel').removeClass('owl-loaded owl-rtl owl-loaded owl-drag');
                $('.list-view').find('.owl-stage-outer').children().unwrap();
                listCarousel();
            });
        }
    });
</script>
